- **`feat_kv_vocab_15k`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_KV_MIN_FREQ=5 EXP_KV_VOCAB=1 EXP_KV_VOCAB_MAX=15000 EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve PR_AUC by enabling key-value vocabulary to capture structured JS config/payload signals that n-grams miss.
- **`feat_textenc_metrics`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_TEXT_ENCODING_FEATURES=1 EXP_TEXT_METRICS_FULL=1 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve PR_AUC by adding text encoding and full text metrics to detect obfuscation and structural anomalies in scripts.
- **`feat_lowfreq_bigrams_trigrams`** `EXP_BIGRAM_MIN_FREQ=50 EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_OBJECTIVE_TRIGRAMS=1 EXP_SUSPICIOUS_TRIGRAMS=1 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve PR_AUC by lowering bigram frequency floor and enabling objective/suspicious trigrams to capture rarer attack patterns.
- **`abl_extreme_blindfold_off`** `EXP_BLINDFOLD=0 EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_EXTREME_FEATURES=0 EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to keep PR_AUC flat or higher by removing noisy extreme and blindfold features that may dilute signal on this route.
- **`trans_xml_tiered_trigrams`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_TIERED_TRIGRAM_MAX=8000 EXP_TIERED_TRIGRAM_MIN_CRIT=2 EXP_TIERED_TRIGRAM_MIN_FREQ=25 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve PR_AUC by transferring XML's successful tiered trigram config to capture severity-weighted path co-occurrences in JS.
- **`trans_ruby_scalepos_sevfrac`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_SCALE_POS_WEIGHT_MULT=0.5 EXP_SEVERITY_FRACTION_FEATURES=1 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve recall@3FPM by combining Ruby's aggressive positive down-weighting with severity fractions to highlight minimal droppers.
- **`gen_kv_seedsearch3`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_KV_MIN_FREQ=5 EXP_KV_VOCAB=1 EXP_KV_VOCAB_MAX=15000 EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_SAVE_ALL_SEEDS=1 EXP_SEED_SEARCH_K=3 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to stabilize PR_AUC gains from kv_vocab by averaging across 3 seeds to distinguish real signal from seed noise.
- **`retry_sevfrac_lowfreq`** `EXP_BIGRAM_MIN_FREQ=100 EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_SEVERITY_FRACTION_FEATURES=1 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to recover PR_AUC from a prior near-winner by retrying severity fractions with low-frequency bigrams on the updated daily corpus.
- **`feat_kv_split_ratios`** `EXP_DISABLE_FEATURE_GROUPS=clusters EXP_KV_VALUE_SPLIT=1 EXP_MAX_TEST_SAMPLES=20000 EXP_METRIC_RATIO_FEATURES=1 EXP_TRAIN_SAMPLES=30000` — Aims to improve recall@3FPM by splitting KV string values into tokens and adding cross-metric ratios to expose hidden payload structure.
